00110: materials={}
00111: batches={}
00112: def material(c):
00113:     key=tuple(c)
00114:     if key not in materials:
00115:         m=bpy.data.materials.new('Finish')
00116:         m.diffuse_color=(*key,1)
00117:         m.use_nodes=True
00118:         bs=m.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00119:         bs.inputs['Base Color'].default_value=(*key,1)
00120:         bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.68
00121:         materials[key]=m
00122:     return materials[key]
00123: 
00124: def addpoly(c,verts,faces):
00125:     key=tuple(c)
00126:     if key not in batches:
00127:         batches[key]=[[],[]]
00128:     v,f=batches[key]
00129:     off=len(v)
00130:     v.extend(verts)
00131:     f.extend([tuple(off+i for i in face) for face in faces])
00132: 
00133: def detailbox(center,size,c,angle=0):
00134:     x,y,z=center
00135:     a,b,d=[s/2 for s in size]
00136:     ca,sa=math.cos(angle),math.sin(angle)
00137:     verts=[]
00138:     for zz in [-d,d]:
00139:         for xx,yy in [(-a,-b),(a,-b),(a,b),(-a,b)]:
00140:             verts.append((x+xx*ca-yy*sa,y+xx*sa+yy*ca,z+zz))
00141:     addpoly(c,verts,[(0,3,2,1),(4,5,6,7),(0,1,5,4),
00142:                      (1,2,6,5),(2,3,7,6),(3,0,4,7)])
00143: 
00144: def cylinder(a,b,r,c,n=12,r2=None):
00145:     a,b=Vector(a),Vector(b)
00146:     axis=(b-a).normalized()
00147:     ref=Vector((0,0,1)) if abs(axis.z)<.9 else Vector((0,1,0))
00148:     u=axis.cross(ref).normalized()
00149:     v=axis.cross(u).normalized()
00150:     rr=r if r2 is None else r2
00151:     verts=[]
00152:     for p,rad in [(a,r),(b,rr)]:
00153:         for i in range(n):
00154:             t=i*2*math.pi/n
00155:             verts.append(tuple(p+rad*(math.cos(t)*u+math.sin(t)*v)))
00156:     faces=[tuple(reversed(range(n))),tuple(range(n,2*n))]
00157:     faces.extend([(i,(i+1)%n,(i+1)%n+n,i+n) for i in range(n)])
00158:     addpoly(c,verts,faces)
00159: 
00160: def wire(name,points,c=black,r=.004):
00161:     cu=bpy.data.curves.new(name,'CURVE')
00162:     cu.dimensions='3D'
00163:     cu.resolution_u=1
00164:     cu.bevel_depth=r
00165:     cu.bevel_resolution=1
00166:     sp=cu.splines.new('POLY')
00167:     sp.points.add(len(points)-1)
00168:     for q,p in zip(sp.points,points):
00169:         q.co=(*p,1)
00170:     ob=bpy.data.objects.new(name,cu)
00171:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(ob)
00172:     cu.materials.append(material(c))
00173:     return ob
00174: 
00175: def rotate_xy(x,y,cx,cy,a):
00176:     dx,dy=x-cx,y-cy
00177:     return (cx+math.cos(a)*dx-math.sin(a)*dy,
00178:             cy+math.sin(a)*dx+math.cos(a)*dy)

00139: def addpoly(c,verts,faces):
00140:     key=tuple(c)
00141:     if key not in batches:
00142:         batches[key]=[[],[]]
00143:     v,f=batches[key]
00144:     off=len(v)
00145:     v.extend(verts)
00146:     f.extend([tuple(off+i for i in face) for face in faces])

00148: def detailbox(center,size,c,angle=0):
00149:     x,y,z=center
00150:     a,b,d=[s/2 for s in size]
00151:     ca,sa=math.cos(angle),math.sin(angle)
00152:     verts=[]
00153:     for zz in [-d,d]:
00154:         for xx,yy in [(-a,-b),(a,-b),(a,b),(-a,b)]:
00155:             verts.append((x+xx*ca-yy*sa,y+xx*sa+yy*ca,z+zz))
00156:     addpoly(c,verts,[(0,3,2,1),(4,5,6,7),(0,1,5,4),(1,2,6,5),(2,3,7,6),(3,0,4,7)])

00158: def cylinder(a,b,r,c,n=12,r2=None):
00159:     a,b=Vector(a),Vector(b)
00160:     axis=(b-a).normalized()
00161:     ref=Vector((0,0,1)) if abs(axis.z)<.9 else Vector((0,1,0))
00162:     u=axis.cross(ref).normalized()
00163:     v=axis.cross(u).normalized()
00164:     rr=r if r2 is None else r2
00165:     verts=[]
00166:     for p,rad in [(a,r),(b,rr)]:
00167:         for i in range(n):
00168:             t=i*2*math.pi/n
00169:             verts.append(tuple(p+rad*(math.cos(t)*u+math.sin(t)*v)))
00170:     faces=[tuple(reversed(range(n))),tuple(range(n,2*n))]
00171:     faces.extend([(i,(i+1)%n,(i+1)%n+n,i+n) for i in range(n)])
00172:     addpoly(c,verts,faces)

00110: materials={}
00111: batches={}
00112: def material(c):
00113:     key=tuple(c)
00114:     if key not in materials:
00115:         m=bpy.data.materials.new('Finish')
00116:         m.diffuse_color=(*key,1)
00117:         m.use_nodes=True
00118:         bs=m.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00119:         bs.inputs['Base Color'].default_value=(*key,1)
00120:         bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.68
00121:         materials[key]=m
00122:     return materials[key]
00123: 
00124: def addpoly(c,verts,faces):
00125:     key=tuple(c)
00126:     if key not in batches:
00127:         batches[key]=[[],[]]
00128:     v,f=batches[key]
00129:     off=len(v)
00130:     v.extend(verts)
00131:     f.extend([tuple(off+i for i in face) for face in faces])
00132: 
00133: def detailbox(center,size,c,angle=0):
00134:     x,y,z=center
00135:     a,b,d=[s/2 for s in size]
00136:     ca,sa=math.cos(angle),math.sin(angle)
00137:     verts=[]
00138:     for zz in [-d,d]:
00139:         for xx,yy in [(-a,-b),(a,-b),(a,b),(-a,b)]:
00140:             verts.append((x+xx*ca-yy*sa,y+xx*sa+yy*ca,z+zz))
00141:     addpoly(c,verts,[(0,3,2,1),(4,5,6,7),(0,1,5,4),
00142:                      (1,2,6,5),(2,3,7,6),(3,0,4,7)])
00143: 
00144: def cylinder(a,b,r,c,n=12,r2=None):
00145:     a,b=Vector(a),Vector(b)
00146:     axis=(b-a).normalized()
00147:     ref=Vector((0,0,1)) if abs(axis.z)<.9 else Vector((0,1,0))
00148:     u=axis.cross(ref).normalized()
00149:     v=axis.cross(u).normalized()
00150:     rr=r if r2 is None else r2
00151:     verts=[]
00152:     for p,rad in [(a,r),(b,rr)]:
00153:         for i in range(n):
00154:             t=i*2*math.pi/n
00155:             verts.append(tuple(p+rad*(math.cos(t)*u+math.sin(t)*v)))
00156:     faces=[tuple(reversed(range(n))),tuple(range(n,2*n))]
00157:     faces.extend([(i,(i+1)%n,(i+1)%n+n,i+n) for i in range(n)])
00158:     addpoly(c,verts,faces)
00159: 
00160: def wire(name,points,c=black,r=.004):
00161:     cu=bpy.data.curves.new(name,'CURVE')
00162:     cu.dimensions='3D'
00163:     cu.resolution_u=1
00164:     cu.bevel_depth=r
00165:     cu.bevel_resolution=1
00166:     sp=cu.splines.new('POLY')
00167:     sp.points.add(len(points)-1)
00168:     for q,p in zip(sp.points,points):
00169:         q.co=(*p,1)
00170:     ob=bpy.data.objects.new(name,cu)
00171:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(ob)
00172:     cu.materials.append(material(c))
00173:     return ob
00174: 
00175: def rotate_xy(x,y,cx,cy,a):
00176:     dx,dy=x-cx,y-cy
00177:     return (cx+math.cos(a)*dx-math.sin(a)*dy,
00178:             cy+math.sin(a)*dx+math.cos(a)*dy)

00055: # Three identical narrow institutional bunks, projecting out from the left wall.
00056: def bunk(name, y, x0=-3.37, x1=-.72, width=.94):
00057:     a=Asset(name)
00058:     ya,yb=y-width/2,y+width/2
00059:     for i,x in enumerate((x0,x1)):
00060:         for j,yy in enumerate((ya,yb)):
00061:             R(a,'post%d%d'%(i,j),.025,(x,yy,.045),(x,yy,2.24))
00062:             a.include('cap%d%d'%(i,j),ball(.031,center=(x,yy,2.24),color=iron))
00063:             B(a,'foot%d%d'%(i,j),(.10,.10,.025),(x,yy,.025),(.15,.16,.14))
00064:     for level,z in enumerate((.43,1.66)):
00065:         B(a,'bedbase%d'%level,(x1-x0+.035,width,.075),((x0+x1)/2,y,z),iron)
00066:         a.include('mattress%d'%level,fillet_box(
00067:             (x1-x0-.045,width-.05,.155),radius=.065,
00068:             center=((x0+x1)/2,y,z+.105),color=(.79,.81,.76)))
00069:         B(a,'blue mattress ticking%d'%level,(x1-x0-.045,width-.045,.047),
00070:           ((x0+x1)/2,y,z+.035),(.22,.34,.47))
00071:         a.include('sheet%d'%level,fillet_box(
00072:             (x1-x0-.10,width-.065,.075),radius=.035,
00073:             center=((x0+x1)/2-.01,y,z+.19),color=(.89,.90,.86)))
00074:         a.include('pillow%d'%level,fillet_box(
00075:             (.50,width*.71,.12),radius=.06,
00076:             center=(x0+.37,y,z+.28),color=(.91,.91,.85)))
00077:         # Slightly rumpled white blanket over the foot.
00078:         blanket=box((.69,width-.045,.055),center=(x1-.43,y,z+.24),
00079:                     color=(.79,.81,.78))
00080:         blanket.turn('y',-2)
00081:         a.include('folded bedding%d'%level,blanket)
00082:         for side,yy in enumerate((ya,yb)):
00083:             R(a,'long edge%d%d'%(level,side),.023,(x0,yy,z+.01),(x1,yy,z+.01))
00084:         # End boards and open tubular guard rails.
00085:         for end,x in enumerate((x0,x1)):

00089:                 B(a,'end dark panel%d'%end,(.035,width-.09,.14),
00090:                   (x,y,z+.36),(.12,.14,.12))
00091:         if level==1:
00092:             for side,yy in enumerate((ya,yb)):

00094:     # Ladder fixed to the foot end.
00095:     for j,yy in enumerate((y-.29,y+.29)):
00096:         R(a,'ladder upright%d'%j,.021,(x1+.055,yy,.12),(x1+.055,yy,1.98))

00215: # Give plaster, oak and stone restrained procedural surface variation.
00216: for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects:
00217:     if obj.type != 'MESH':
00218:         continue
00219:     for mat in obj.data.materials:
00220:         if not mat:
00221:             continue
00222:         mat.use_nodes=True
00223:         bs=mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00224:         if bs:
00225:             bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.79
00226:             name=obj.name.lower()
00227:             if any(word in name for word in ('board','plaster','ceiling','jamb','lintel','timber','upright','cornice','panel')):
00228:                 nodes=mat.node_tree.nodes
00229:                 links=mat.node_tree.links
00230:                 tex=nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00231:                 tex.inputs['Scale'].default_value=19
00232:                 tex.inputs['Detail'].default_value=3
00233:                 bump=nodes.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00234:                 bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=.17
00235:                 bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=.035
00236:                 links.new(tex.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00237:                 links.new(bump.outputs['Normal'],bs.inputs['Normal'])
